## docs/design-resources/wechat-miniapp/shared/liquid-glass/material.mjs
// Editable design experiment, not a WEAPP renderer. Optical reference: README.
const ns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg';let sequence=0;
export function mountLiquidGlass(element,{theme='light'}={}){
 element.dataset.liquidGlass=theme;
 const svg=document.createElementNS(ns,'svg');svg.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');svg.style.cssText='position:absolute;width:0;height:0;pointer-events:none';
 const id='starward-glass-'+(++sequence);
 svg.innerHTML='<filter id="'+id+'" x="0%" y="0%" width="100%" height="100%" color-interpolation-filters="sRGB"><feImage x="0" y="0" width="100%" height="100%" preserveAspectRatio="none" result="map"/><feDisplacementMap in="SourceGraphic" in2="map" scale="24" xChannelSelector="R" yChannelSelector="G"/></filter>';
 document.body.append(svg);const image=svg.querySelector('feImage');let key='',raf=0,mode='refractive',disposed=false;
 const enhanced=/Chrome\/|Chromium\//.test(navigator.userAgent)&&CSS.supports('backdrop-filter','url("#'+id+'")');
 const applyMode=()=>{element.dataset.glassMode=mode==='refractive'&&!enhanced?'translucent':mode;
  if(mode==='refractive'&&enhanced)element.style.setProperty('--glass-displacement','url("#'+id+'")');else element.style.removeProperty('--glass-displacement');};
 const update=()=>{
  if(disposed)return;const box=element.getBoundingClientRect();if(box.width<1||box.height<1)return;
  const radius=parseFloat(getComputedStyle(element).borderTopLeftRadius)||20,w=Math.ceil(box.width),h=Math.ceil(box.height),next=[w,h,radius].join(':');if(next===key)return;key=next;
  const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=w;c.height=h;const ctx=c.getContext('2d'),data=ctx.createImageData(w,h),rimData=ctx.createImageData(w,h),r=Math.min(radius,w/2,h/2),bevel=Math.min(16,r),depth=7,base=3,ior=1.5;
  // Convex fourth-order cross-section, Snell bending through the top surface,
  // projected to a background plane: a bounded single-interface approximation.
  // Foreground text never enters the filter. No scene screenshots/per-frame copies.
  const shifts=Array.from({length:257},(_,i)=>{const t=Math.max(.0001,i/256),u=1-t,a=1-u**4,height=a**.25,slope=depth/bevel*u**3*a**(-.75),theta=Math.atan(slope),bend=theta-Math.asin(Math.sin(theta)/ior);return Math.min(12,(base+depth*height)*Math.tan(bend));});
  for(let y=0;y<h;y++)for(let x=0;x<w;x++){
   const px=x+.5-w/2,py=y+.5-h/2,qx=Math.abs(px)-(w/2-r),qy=Math.abs(py)-(h/2-r),ox=Math.max(qx,0),oy=Math.max(qy,0),len=Math.hypot(ox,oy),distance=len+Math.min(Math.max(qx,qy),0)-r;
   let nx=0,ny=0;if(len){nx=ox/len*Math.sign(px);ny=oy/len*Math.sign(py);}else if(qx>qy)nx=Math.sign(px);else ny=Math.sign(py);
   const inset=Math.max(0,-distance),shift=distance<=0&&inset<bevel?shifts[Math.round(inset/bevel*256)]:0,i=(y*w+x)*4;
   data.data[i]=Math.round(255*(.5-nx*shift/24));data.data[i+1]=Math.round(255*(.5-ny*shift/24));data.data[i+2]=128;data.data[i+3]=255;
   // Directional rim follows the same normals, with a weaker secondary reflection.
   const light=-.447*nx-.894*ny,shine=.035+.82*Math.max(0,light)**4+.17*Math.max(0,-light)**9,band=Math.exp(-(((inset-.55)/.66)**2))+.14*Math.exp(-(((inset-1.6)/1.6)**2));
   rimData.data[i]=rimData.data[i+1]=rimData.data[i+2]=255;rimData.data[i+3]=distance<=0?Math.round(255*Math.min(1,shine*band)):0;
  }
  ctx.putImageData(data,0,0);image.setAttribute('href',c.toDataURL());ctx.putImageData(rimData,0,0);element.style.setProperty('--glass-rim-map','url("'+c.toDataURL()+'")');applyMode();
 };
 const observer=new ResizeObserver(()=>{cancelAnimationFrame(raf);raf=requestAnimationFrame(update);});observer.observe(element);update();
 return {setMode(value){mode=value;applyMode();},destroy(){if(disposed)return;disposed=true;observer.disconnect();cancelAnimationFrame(raf);svg.remove();element.style.removeProperty('--glass-displacement');element.style.removeProperty('--glass-rim-map');delete element.dataset.liquidGlass;delete element.dataset.glassMode;}};
}
const instances=[...document.querySelectorAll('[data-liquid-glass]')].map(e=>mountLiquidGlass(e,{theme:e.dataset.liquidGlass}));
addEventListener('pagehide',e=>{if(!e.persisted)instances.forEach(i=>i.destroy());});

## HTML

## docs/design-resources/wechat-miniapp/contributions/adopted/creation-feedback/preview/app.mjs
import {spotCard} from '/shared/spot-card/card.mjs';
import {names,seed,mutate} from './model.mjs';
import {changes} from '/feedback/adopted/spot-feedback/preview/diff.mjs';
const $=s=>document.querySelector(s),clone=structuredClone;
const base=await fetch('/feedback/adopted/spot-feedback/baseline.json').then(r=>r.json());
let records=seed(base),group='NEW',filters={NEW:'ALL',FEEDBACK:'ALL'},scrolls={NEW:0,FEEDBACK:0},active=null,scenario='normal';
const stateIcon=s=>'<svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true">'+(s==='PENDING'?'<circle cx="10" cy="10" r="7"/><path d="M10 5v5l3 2"/>':s==='REJECTED'?'<circle cx="10" cy="10" r="7"/><path d="M10 5v6m0 2v1"/>':s==='DRAFT'?'<path d="m4 12 8-8 4 4-8 8-5 1zM10 6l4 4"/>':'<circle cx="10" cy="10" r="7"/><path d="m6 10 3 3 5-6"/>')+'</svg>';
const art=(r)=>'<svg viewBox="0 0 52 52" aria-hidden="true"><defs><linearGradient id="pin-'+r.id+'" x2=".8" y2="1"><stop stop-color="'+(r.status==='DRAFT'?'#c5ced0':'#a4bac1')+'"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="'+(r.status==='DRAFT'?'#8f9ca4':'#637f8c')+'"/></linearGradient></defs><ellipse cx="26" cy="44" rx="12" ry="3" fill="#627c8116"/><path d="M26 43S12 30 12 21a14 14 0 1 1 28 0c0 9-14 22-14 22Z" fill="url(#pin-'+r.id+')" stroke="#fff9" stroke-width="1.1"/><circle cx="26" cy="21" r="9" fill="#edf2ee"/><path d="m26 13 2.4 5 5.6.8-4 4 1 5.6-5-2.7-5 2.7 1-5.6-4-4 5.6-.8Z" fill="'+(r.status==='DRAFT'?'#afbbc0':'#c7ac70')+'"/>'+(r.kind==='FEEDBACK'?'<path d="m34 35 8-8 4 4-8 8-5 1Z" fill="#dac99d" stroke="#fff"/>':'')+'</svg>';
const esc=s=>String(s??'').replace(/[&<>"]/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c]));
function recordMarkup(r,i){
 const summary=r.kind==='FEEDBACK'?'<div class="field-summary">'+changes(r.baseline,r.payload).map(c=>'<span>'+esc(({hours:'开放时间',parkingNote:'停车说明',detail:'补充说明',name:'地点名称',address:'地点地址',road:'末段道路',safety:'夜间安全',parking:'停车照片',toilet:'洗手间照片',site:'现场照片'}[c.key]||'场地信息'))+'</span>').join('')+'</div>':'';
 const card=spotCard({name:r.payload.fields.name,region:r.presentation?.region,address:r.payload.point?.address||r.payload.fields.address||'尚未选择地址',photo:r.payload.photos.find(p=>p.kind==='site')?.url});
 const date=new Intl.DateTimeFormat('zh-CN',{month:'2-digit',day:'2-digit',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit',hour12:false}).format(new Date(r.updatedAt));
 const timeLabel=r.status==='DRAFT'?'保存':r.status==='REJECTED'?'审核':r.status==='PUBLISHED'?'上线':r.status==='APPROVED'?'通过':'提交';
 const action=r.status==='DRAFT'?'继续编辑':r.status==='REJECTED'?'修改后再提交':r.status==='PUBLISHED'?'查看观星点':'查看'+(r.kind==='FEEDBACK'?'本次反馈':'提交内容');
 return '<button class="record '+r.status+'" data-id="'+r.id+'" style="animation-delay:'+Math.min(i*20,60)+'ms">'+card+'<div class="record-review"><div class="record-meta"><span class="badge '+r.status+'">'+stateIcon(r.status)+names[r.status]+'</span><time>'+date+' '+timeLabel+'</time></div>'+summary+(r.reason?'<p class="reason">'+esc(r.reason)+'</p>':'')+'<div class="record-bottom"><span class="record-action">'+action+' ↗</span></div></div></button>';
}
function alignTab(){const tab=document.querySelector('[data-group="'+group+'"]');if(tab)document.querySelector('.groups>i').style.transform='translateX('+(tab.offsetLeft+(tab.offsetWidth-29)/2)+'px)';}
function render(){const rows=records.filter(r=>r.kind===group).sort((a,b)=>Date.parse(b.updatedAt)-Date.parse(a.updatedAt));$('.groups').dataset.feedback=String(group==='FEEDBACK');document.querySelectorAll('[data-group]').forEach(b=>b.setAttribute('aria-selected',String(b.dataset.group===group)));
 const states=group==='NEW'?['ALL','DRAFT','PENDING','PUBLISHED','REJECTED']:['ALL','PENDING','APPROVED','REJECTED'];$('#filters').innerHTML=states.map(s=>'<button data-status="'+s+'" aria-pressed="'+(filters[group]===s)+'"><span>'+(s==='ALL'?'全部':names[s])+'</span></button>').join('');
 const shown=scenario==='empty'?[]:rows.filter(r=>filters[group]==='ALL'||r.status===filters[group]);
 $('#records').innerHTML=scenario==='error'?'<div class="empty"><strong>暂时未能读取记录</strong><p>已保存的资料不会丢失</p><button id="retry">重试</button></div>':shown.length?shown.map(recordMarkup).join(''):'<div class="empty">'+art({id:'empty',status:'DRAFT',kind:group})+'<strong>暂无'+(filters[group]==='ALL'?'记录':names[filters[group]]+'记录')+'</strong><p>'+(group==='NEW'?'在地图上添加的观星点会出现在这里':'提交的观星点反馈会出现在这里')+'</p></div>';
 document.querySelectorAll('[data-status]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{filters[group]=b.dataset.status;scrolls[group]=0;render();});document.querySelectorAll('[data-id]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>openRecord(b.dataset.id));$('#retry')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{scenario='normal';render();});$('#records').scrollTop=scrolls[group];alignTab();}
function close(){const previous=active;active=null;$('#editor').hidden=true;$('#location').hidden=true;$('#list-page').inert=false;document.querySelector('#phone>header').inert=false;render();if(previous)document.querySelector('[data-id="'+previous+'"]')?.focus({preventScroll:true});}
async function openRecord(id,replace=false){const r=records.find(x=>x.id===id);if(!r)return;scrolls[group]=$('#records').scrollTop;active=id;$('#list-page').inert=true;document.querySelector('#phone>header').inert=true;
 if(r.status==='PUBLISHED'){const f=$('#location iframe');$('#location').hidden=false;f.onload=()=>{const d=f.contentDocument;d.querySelector('#extent-panel h2').textContent=r.payload.fields.name;};f.src='/map/adopted/spot-information/preview/index.html?extent=medium';if(!replace)history.pushState({record:id},'');return;}
 $('#editor').hidden=false;const f=$('#editor iframe');const source='/feedback/adopted/spot-feedback/preview/';let html=await fetch(source+'index.html').then(r=>r.text());html=html.replace('<head>','<head><base href="'+source+'">').replace('src="editor.mjs"','src="/contributions/adopted/creation-feedback/preview/host.mjs"');f.srcdoc=html;if(!replace)history.pushState({record:id},'');}
// The shared editor runs inside this same-origin preview iframe. This store is session-only demo data.
window.creationReview={record:()=>clone(records.find(r=>r.id===active)),close:()=>history.back(),async api(path,body){if(path==='state'){const r=records.find(x=>x.id===active);return{records:clone(records),spot:clone(r?.baseline||base),revision:r?.baseRevision||1};}
 await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,650));if(scenario==='submit-error')throw Error('提交失败，请重试。修改内容已保留。');const old=records.find(r=>r.id===active);const next=mutate(old,path,body);records=records.map(r=>r.id===active?next:r);return clone(next);},completed(r){openRecord(r.id,true);}};
$('#back').onclick=()=>{if(active)history.back();else{parent.postMessage({type:'creation-review-back'},location.origin);if(parent===window)location.href='/my/adopted/my-page/preview/index.html';}};
$('#location-back').onclick=()=>history.back();addEventListener('popstate',close);document.querySelectorAll('[data-group]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{scrolls[group]=$('#records').scrollTop;group=b.dataset.group;render();});
addEventListener('message',e=>{if(e.source!==parent||e.origin!==location.origin||e.data?.type!=='creation-scenario')return;const s=e.data.value;if(s==='reset'){records=seed(base);filters={NEW:'ALL',FEEDBACK:'ALL'};scenario='normal';group='NEW';close();}else if(s==='NEW'||s==='FEEDBACK'){group=s;scenario='normal';close();}else if(s==='n-rejected'||s==='f-rejected'){group=s.startsWith('n-')?'NEW':'FEEDBACK';scenario='normal';openRecord(s);}else{scenario=s;close();}render();});render();

document.querySelector('.groups').addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(!['ArrowLeft','ArrowRight'].includes(e.key))return;e.preventDefault();const next=group==='NEW'?'FEEDBACK':'NEW';const button=document.querySelector('[data-group="'+next+'"]');button.click();button.focus();});

new ResizeObserver(alignTab).observe(document.querySelector('.groups'));document.fonts.ready.then(alignTab);

## docs/design-resources/wechat-miniapp/feedback/adopted/spot-feedback/preview/editor.mjs
import './choices.mjs';
import './chapters.mjs';
import {createMap} from './map.mjs';
import {changes} from './diff.mjs';
const $=s=>document.querySelector(s), phone=$('#phone'),form=$('#spot-form'),sheet=$('#add-sheet');
const host=window.contributionPreviewHost;
const account=new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('account')||'preview-owner';
let records=[],baseline=null,baseRevision=1,current=null,selected=null,mode='new',point=null,photos=[],busy=false,opened=false,frozen=false,initial=null,token=0;
const labels=Object.fromEntries([...form.elements].filter(e=>e.name).map(e=>[e.name,e.getAttribute('aria-label')||e.name]));
const photoLabels={parking:'停车照片',toilet:'洗手间照片',site:'现场照片'};
const map=createMap({onSelect:r=>showInfo(r),onTap:()=>{if(!opened)hideInfo();}});
const tools=[...document.querySelectorAll('[data-purpose=floating-map-tools] button')];
async function api(path,body){if(host)return host.api(path,body);const response=await fetch('/api/design/'+path,{method:body?'POST':'GET',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json','X-Demo-Account':account},...(body?{body:JSON.stringify(body)}:{})});const data=await response.json();if(!response.ok)throw Error(data.error||'请求失败');return data;}
function savedAt(value){if(!value)return '';const date=new Date(value),now=new Date();return(date.toDateString()===now.toDateString()?'':date.toLocaleDateString('zh-CN')+' ')+date.toLocaleTimeString('zh-CN',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'})+' 已保存';}
function error(text){$('#form-error').textContent=text;$('#form-error').hidden=!text;}
function payload(){const fields={};for(const e of form.elements)if(e.name&&e.type!=='checkbox')fields[e.name]=e.value;return{fields,point,photos};}
function setBusy(value){busy=value;phone.dataset.busy=String(value);map.setLocked(value);form.setAttribute('aria-busy',String(value));for(const e of form.elements)e.disabled=value||frozen;document.querySelectorAll('.choice-group button').forEach(e=>e.disabled=value||frozen);$('#close-sheet').disabled=value;$('#submit').innerHTML=value?'<span class="spinner"></span> 提交中…':host?.submitLabel||(mode==='feedback'?'提交反馈':'提交审核');$('#save-draft').disabled=value;}
function renderMarker(){const el=$('#candidate');el.hidden=!point;el.querySelector('.font-medium').textContent=form.elements.name.value.trim()||'未命名观星点';$('#selected-address').textContent=point?.address||'选择地址后确定地图位置';}
function renderPhotos(){for(const kind of ['parking','toilet','site']){const list=$('#photo-list-'+kind);list.replaceChildren();photos.forEach((p,i)=>{if(p.kind!==kind)return;const cell=document.createElement('div'),img=document.createElement('img');img.src=p.url;img.alt=p.name||'地点照片';cell.append(img);if(!frozen){const del=document.createElement('button');del.type='button';del.textContent='×';del.ariaLabel='移除'+(p.name||'照片');del.onclick=()=>{photos.splice(i,1);renderPhotos();renderDiff();};cell.append(del);}list.append(cell);});list.closest('.media-group').querySelector('.photo-upload').hidden=frozen;}
 $('#photo-rights').hidden=frozen||!photos.some(p=>p.url.startsWith('data:'));
}
function renderDiff(){document.querySelectorAll('.changed').forEach(e=>e.classList.remove('changed'));const list=$('#change-list');list.replaceChildren();$('#changes').hidden=mode!=='feedback';if(mode!=='feedback')return;for(const change of changes(initial,payload())){if(change.kind==='text'){const input=form.elements.namedItem(change.key);(input?.closest('.field-row,.address-field')||input)?.classList.add('changed');}else $('#photo-list-'+change.key).closest('.media-group').classList.add('changed');const row=document.createElement('div');row.className='delta';const label=document.createElement('strong');label.textContent=(change.kind==='photos'?photoLabels:labels)[change.key]||change.key;row.append(label);const line=document.createElement('div');line.className='delta-line';const arrow=document.createElement('span');arrow.className='arrow';arrow.textContent='→';
 if(change.kind==='text'){const old=document.createElement('del'),next=document.createElement('span');old.textContent=change.before||'未填写';next.textContent=change.after||'已清空';line.append(old,arrow,next);}else{for(const [index,group]of [change.before,change.after].entries()){if(index)line.append(arrow);if(!group.length){const none=document.createElement('span');none.textContent='无图片';line.append(none);}for(const p of group){const box=document.createElement('span'),img=document.createElement('img');box.className=index?'new-photo':'old-photo';img.src=p.url;img.alt=(index?'新图：':'原图：')+p.name;box.append(img);line.append(box);}}}row.append(line);list.append(row);}}
function fill(data){for(const e of form.elements)if(e.name){if(e.type==='checkbox')e.checked=false;else e.value=data.fields?.[e.name]??'';}point=structuredClone(data.point||null);photos=structuredClone(data.photos||[]);document.dispatchEvent(new Event('draft-restored'));renderMarker();renderPhotos();renderDiff();}
function hideInfo(){token++;selected=null;$('#spot-presentation').hidden=true;phone.dataset.spotOpen='false';map.clear();}
function closeEditor(force=false){if(busy)return false;if(!force&&!frozen&&initial&&JSON.stringify(payload())!==JSON.stringify(host?.editStart||initial)&&!window.confirm('修改尚未提交或保存，关闭后将丢弃本次修改。'))return false;opened=false;phone.dataset.open='false';sheet.inert=true;sheet.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');phone.dataset.mode='map';if(host&&!force)host.close();return true;}
async function loadState(){const result=await api('state');records=result.records;baseline=result.spot;baseRevision=result.revision;map.replaceUserRecords(records.filter(r=>r.kind==='NEW').map((r,i)=>({id:r.id,name:r.payload.fields.name,address:r.payload.point?.address||'',x:(i%3-1)*95,y:80+Math.floor(i/3)*54,status:r.status,record:r})));}
function openEditor(nextMode,data,record=null,readonly=false){hideInfo();mode=nextMode;current=record;frozen=readonly;initial=structuredClone(nextMode==='feedback'?(record?.baseline||baseline):data);phone.dataset.mode=nextMode==='feedback'?'feedback':'new';phone.dataset.frozen=String(frozen);$('#save-draft').hidden=nextMode==='feedback';$('#changes').hidden=nextMode!=='feedback';$('#save-status').replaceChildren();$('#save-status').textContent=readonly?'审核中':record?.status==='DRAFT'?savedAt(record.updatedAt):'';$('#save-status').classList.toggle('review-tag',readonly);sheet.setAttribute('aria-label',nextMode==='feedback'?'观星点反馈':'观星点编辑');sheet.querySelector('h2').textContent=nextMode==='feedback'?baseline.fields.name+'反馈页':record?'编辑观星点':'新增观星点';$('#close-sheet').ariaLabel='关闭编辑页面';opened=true;phone.dataset.open='true';sheet.inert=false;sheet.setAttribute('aria-hidden','false');fill(data);if(nextMode!=='feedback')initial=structuredClone(payload());document.title=sheet.querySelector('h2').textContent;setBusy(false);error('');map.recenter();$('.form-scroll').scrollTop=0;host?.decorate({record,readonly,mode});}
function newEditor(){openEditor('new',{fields:{},point:null,photos:[]});}
async function feedbackEditor(){try{await loadState();const pending=records.find(r=>r.kind==='FEEDBACK'&&r.spot==='bay'&&r.status==='PENDING');openEditor('feedback',pending?.payload||baseline,pending,!!pending);}catch(e){error(e.message);}}
function showInfo(record){if(opened&&!closeEditor())return;selected=record;const mine=record.id==='bay'?records.find(r=>r.kind==='FEEDBACK'&&r.spot==='bay'&&r.status==='PENDING'):null;const ticket=++token;phone.dataset.spotOpen='true';const frame=$('#spot-frame'),holder=$('#spot-presentation');holder.hidden=true;map.focus(record.id,414);setTimeout(()=>{if(ticket!==token)return;holder.hidden=false;const sync=()=>{if(ticket!==token)return;const d=frame.contentDocument,panel=d?.querySelector('#extent-panel');if(!panel)return;panel.style.borderRadius='0';panel.style.boxShadow='none';const title=panel.querySelector('h2');title.textContent=record.name;title.parentElement.style.gap='8px';const region=title.parentElement.querySelector('span');if(region)region.style.marginLeft='auto';const tagCSS=d.createElement('style');tagCSS.textContent='.my-feedback-tag:after{content:"";position:absolute;inset:-12px -2px;min-height:44px}';d.head.append(tagCSS);const data=record.record?.payload||baseline;const addr=panel.querySelector('.location-facts');if(addr?.firstChild)addr.firstChild.textContent=(data.point?.address||record.address)+' ';const makeTag=(text,click)=>{const tag=d.createElement(click?'button':'span');tag.textContent=text;tag.style.cssText='margin-left:8px;background:#faf2df;color:#98783e;padding:3px 6px;font-size:10px;border-radius:4px;vertical-align:middle;white-space:nowrap';if(click){tag.className='my-feedback-tag';tag.style.position='relative';tag.style.zIndex='8';tag.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>e.stopPropagation());tag.onclick=e=>{e.stopPropagation();click();};}title.append(tag);};if(mine){makeTag('我的反馈',()=>openEditor('feedback',mine.payload,mine,true));const clock=d.createElement('span');clock.textContent='◷';clock.ariaLabel='审核中';clock.style.cssText='color:#ac8a47;font-size:18px;margin-left:0';title.parentElement.append(clock);}else if(record.status==='PENDING')makeTag('审核中');else if(record.status==='DRAFT')makeTag('草稿');
 const hours=d.querySelector('.hours-row>span:last-child');if(hours)hours.textContent=data.fields.hours||'暂无数据';const safety=d.querySelectorAll('.access-compact dd');['openness','access','safety'].forEach((key,i)=>{if(safety[i])safety[i].textContent=data.fields[key]||'不清楚';});const note=d.querySelector('.access-compact p');if(note)note.textContent=data.fields.accessNote||'';const source=d.querySelector('[data-target=feedback]');source.textContent='我要反馈 ↗';source.style.cssText='border:0;background:transparent;color:#748191;padding:0 0 0 8px;min-height:44px;font-size:12px';source.hidden=record.id!=='bay';if(record.record){const attribution=source.parentElement.querySelector('span');if(attribution)attribution.textContent=record.status==='DRAFT'?'我的草稿':'待审核资料';const elevation=d.querySelector('.location-facts span');if(elevation)elevation.textContent='海拔 暂无数据';const route=d.querySelector('.route-row h3');if(route)route.textContent='路线 暂无数据';const road=d.querySelector('.route-row p');if(road)road.textContent=data.fields.road||'末段道路 暂无数据';}source.onclick=e=>{e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();feedbackEditor();};source.addEventListener('click',source.onclick,{capture:true,once:true});
 for(const action of d.querySelectorAll('#fixed-actions>div>button:not([data-target=sky])'))action.style.display=record.record?'none':'';
 const sky=d.querySelector('[data-target=sky]');if(sky&&record.status==='DRAFT'){sky.textContent='编辑';sky.addEventListener('click',e=>{e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();openEditor('new',record.record.payload,record.record);},{capture:true});}
 const lane=d.querySelector('#fixed-actions>div');if(lane)lane.style.gridTemplateColumns=record.record?'1fr':'';
 holder.style.height=panel.getBoundingClientRect().height+'px';frame.style.bottom='-68.8px';const observer=new ResizeObserver(()=>{if(ticket!==token){observer.disconnect();return;}holder.style.height=panel.getBoundingClientRect().height+'px';});observer.observe(panel);};frame.onload=sync;frame.src='/map/adopted/spot-information/preview/index.html?extent=medium&v='+ticket;},300);}
tools[2].onclick=newEditor;tools[0].onclick=()=>map.recenter();tools[1].onclick=()=>{};
$('#close-sheet').onclick=()=>closeEditor();document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Escape'){if(opened)closeEditor();else hideInfo();}});
const examples=[{id:'east',address:'东岸路观景步道入口',label:'东岸观景台',x:0,y:0},{id:'hill',address:'山脊步道北入口',label:'山顶平台',x:40,y:0}];
function suggestions(){if(frozen)return;const list=$('#address-results');list.replaceChildren();for(const p of examples){const b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.textContent=p.address;b.onclick=()=>{point={...p};form.elements.address.value=p.address;list.hidden=true;renderMarker();renderDiff();map.recenter();};list.append(b);}list.hidden=false;}
$('#address').onfocus=suggestions;$('#address').oninput=()=>{point=null;renderMarker();renderDiff();};$('#clear-address').onclick=()=>{form.elements.address.value='';point=null;renderMarker();renderDiff();suggestions();};form.addEventListener('input',()=>{renderMarker();renderDiff();});document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{if(!e.target.closest('.address-field,#address-results'))$('#address-results').hidden=true;});
for(const upload of document.querySelectorAll('[data-photo-kind]')){const label=upload.closest('label');label.tabIndex=0;label.setAttribute('role','button');label.onkeydown=e=>{if(!frozen&&!busy&&['Enter',' '].includes(e.key)){e.preventDefault();upload.click();}};upload.onchange=async e=>{try{for(const file of e.target.files){if(file.size>300000||photos.length>=9){error('演示图片限单张300KB、最多9张');break;}if(!file.type.startsWith('image/'))continue;const url=await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const reader=new FileReader();reader.onload=()=>resolve(reader.result);reader.onerror=reject;reader.readAsDataURL(file);});photos.push({id:crypto.randomUUID(),url,name:file.name,kind:upload.dataset.photoKind});}}catch(e){error('图片读取失败，请重新选择');}upload.value='';renderPhotos();renderDiff();};}
$('#save-draft').onclick=async()=>{if(busy||mode==='feedback')return;setBusy(true);$('#save-draft').textContent='保存中…';try{const record=await api('draft',{data:payload(),id:current?.id,revision:current?.rev});current=record;initial=structuredClone(payload());if(host)host.editStart=structuredClone(payload());await loadState();sheet.querySelector('h2').textContent='编辑观星点';$('#save-status').textContent=savedAt(record.updatedAt);error('');}catch(e){error(e.message);}finally{$('#save-draft').textContent='存草稿';setBusy(false);}};
form.onsubmit=async e=>{e.preventDefault();if(busy||frozen)return;if(mode==='feedback'&&!changes(initial,payload()).length){error('尚未修改任何信息');return;}if(photos.some(p=>p.url.startsWith('data:'))&&!form.elements.rights.checked){error('请确认照片使用权');return;}if(mode!=='feedback'&&!form.elements.consent.checked){error('请确认位置用于审核');return;}setBusy(true);try{const record=await api(mode==='feedback'?'feedback':'new',{data:payload(),id:current?.id,revision:current?.rev,baseRevision});setBusy(false);closeEditor(true);await loadState();if(host){host.completed(record);return;}showInfo(record.kind==='FEEDBACK'?{id:'bay',name:baseline.fields.name,address:baseline.point.address}:{id:record.id,name:record.payload.fields.name,address:record.payload.point.address,status:record.status,record});}catch(e){error(e.message);setBusy(false);}};
try{await loadState();if(host){const r=records.find(r=>r.id===host.recordId);if(!r)throw Error('记录不存在');host.editStart=structuredClone(r.payload);openEditor(r.kind==='FEEDBACK'?'feedback':'new',r.payload,r,!['DRAFT','REJECTED'].includes(r.status));}else if(new URLSearchParams(location.search).has('new'))newEditor();else if(new URLSearchParams(location.search).has('map')){closeEditor(true);showInfo({id:'bay',name:baseline.fields.name,address:baseline.point.address});}else await feedbackEditor();}catch(e){const p=document.createElement('p');p.id='service-state';p.textContent='预览服务连接失败，请启动本机4284设计服务后刷新。';phone.append(p);}

## docs/design-resources/wechat-miniapp/shared/observation-time/control.js
/* Browser design-resource component. Production uses Taro and ObservationContext. */
(() => {
  const calendarIcon = '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.7" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="3" y="5" width="18" height="16" rx="3"/><path d="M7 2v6M17 2v6M3 11h18"/></svg>';
  window.StarwardObservationTime = {
    mount(root, { days, dayIndex = 7, index = 6, moonSource, onChange = () => {} }) {
      let selectedDay = dayIndex, selectedIndex = index, drag = null, suppressClick = false;
      let presented = index, motionFrame = 0;
      const reducedMotion = matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)');
      const shiftDate = (date, offset) => new Date(Date.parse(date + 'T12:00:00Z') + offset * 86400000).toISOString().slice(0,10);
      const dateOf = (day, row) => row.localDate || shiftDate(day.date, row.next ? 1 : 0);
      const dateLabel = date => date.slice(5,7) + '月' + date.slice(8,10) + '日';
      const samples = days.flatMap((day, dayIndex) => day.hours.map((row, index) => ({date:dateOf(day,row),dayIndex,index,clock:row.at})));
      const dates = [...new Set(samples.map(s => s.date))].sort();
      const listeners = new AbortController();
      const listen = (el, type, fn, options = {}) => el.addEventListener(type, fn, { ...options, signal: listeners.signal });
      const clamp = n => Math.max(0, Math.min(days[selectedDay].hours.length - 1, n));
      root.classList.add('observation-time');
      root.dataset.moon = String(Boolean(moonSource));
      root.innerHTML = '<div class="date-bar"><button id="date-prev" type="button" aria-label="前一天">‹</button><button id="date-open" type="button" aria-haspopup="dialog"><span id="date-label"></span>' + calendarIcon + '</button><button id="date-next" type="button" aria-label="后一天">›</button><button id="date-today" type="button">今晚</button></div><div id="time-ruler" role="slider" tabindex="0" aria-label="观测时间"><div id="time-track"></div><span class="time-center" aria-hidden="true"></span></div><p id="night-time-label" aria-live="polite"></p>';
      const get = id => root.querySelector('#' + id), ruler = get('time-ruler'), track = get('time-track');
      const calendar = document.createElement('dialog');
      calendar.className = 'observation-calendar'; calendar.id = 'date-picker'; calendar.setAttribute('aria-labelledby', 'calendar-title');
      calendar.innerHTML = '<header><h2 id="calendar-title">选择日期</h2><button id="calendar-close" type="button" aria-label="关闭日期选择">×</button></header><p id="calendar-month"></p><div class="calendar-week">' + ['一','二','三','四','五','六','日'].map(t => '<span>' + t + '</span>').join('') + '</div><div id="calendar-days" role="group" aria-label="日期"></div><p class="calendar-note">日期按地点当地时间显示；灰色日期暂无可选时刻。</p>';
      document.body.append(calendar);
      const grid = calendar.querySelector('#calendar-days');
      let shownMonth = '';
      function state() { const day = days[selectedDay], row = day.hours[selectedIndex]; return { dayIndex:selectedDay, index:selectedIndex, day, row, localDate:dateOf(day,row) }; }
      function paint(progress) {
        presented = progress;
        track.style.transform = `translateX(${-33 - progress * 66}px)`;
        [...track.children].forEach((b, j) => {
          const distance = Math.abs(j - progress);
          b.style.transform = `translateY(${Math.min(12, distance * distance * 3)}px)`;
          b.style.opacity = String(Math.max(.25, 1 - distance * .22));
        });
      }
      function stopMotion() { cancelAnimationFrame(motionFrame); motionFrame = 0; root.dataset.settling = 'false'; }
      function settle(target) {
        stopMotion();
        const start = presented, startAt = performance.now();
        if (reducedMotion.matches || Math.abs(target-start) < .001) { paint(target); return; }
        root.dataset.settling = 'true';
        function tick(now) {
          const t = Math.min(1, (now-startAt)/220);
          paint(start + (target-start) * (1-Math.pow(1-t,3)));
          if (t < 1) motionFrame = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
          else { motionFrame = 0; root.dataset.settling = 'false'; }
        }
        motionFrame = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      }
      function calendarMonth() {
        const month = state().localDate.slice(0, 7);
        if (month === shownMonth) return;
        shownMonth = month;
        const [year, number] = month.split('-').map(Number);
        calendar.querySelector('#calendar-month').textContent = `${year}年${number}月 · 地点当地时间`;
        grid.replaceChildren();
        const weekday = (new Date(Date.UTC(year, number - 1, 1)).getUTCDay() + 6) % 7;
        for (let n = 0; n < weekday; n++) grid.append(document.createElement('span'));
        const total = new Date(Date.UTC(year, number, 0)).getUTCDate();
        for (let n = 1; n <= total; n++) {
          const date = `${month}-${String(n).padStart(2, '0')}`;
          const b = document.createElement('button'); b.type = 'button'; b.textContent = n; b.disabled = !dates.includes(date); b.dataset.date = date;
          b.setAttribute('aria-label', `${number}月${n}日`);
          if (days.some(d => d.date === date && d.offset === 0)) { const today = document.createElement('small'); today.textContent = '今天'; b.append(today); }
          grid.append(b);
        }
      }
      function render() {
        const {day, row, localDate} = state();
        const week = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('zh-CN', {weekday:'short', timeZone:'UTC'}).format(new Date(localDate + 'T12:00:00Z'));
        get('date-label').replaceChildren(document.createTextNode(dateLabel(localDate)), Object.assign(document.createElement('span'), {className:'date-weekday', textContent:' ' + week}));
        get('date-open').setAttribute('aria-label', `${dateLabel(localDate)} ${week}，选择日期`);
        get('date-prev').disabled = localDate === dates[0];
        get('date-next').disabled = localDate === dates[dates.length-1];
        get('date-today').hidden = day.offset === 0;
        get('night-time-label').textContent = row.next ? day.label + '观测夜' : (day.offset < 0 ? '历史时段' : day.offset === 0 ? '今晚' : '观测夜');
        if (track.dataset.date !== day.date) {
          track.dataset.date = day.date; track.replaceChildren();
          day.hours.forEach((r, i) => {
            const b = document.createElement('button'); b.type = 'button'; b.dataset.time = i; b.tabIndex = -1;
            b.innerHTML = '<i aria-hidden="true"></i><span></span>';
            b.querySelector('span').textContent = r.at;
            if (moonSource) { const moon = document.createElement('img'); moon.className = 'tick-moon'; moon.alt = ''; moon.src = moonSource(r); b.append(moon); }
            b.setAttribute('aria-label', (r.next ? '次日 ' : '') + r.at + (moonSource ? '，' + r.phaseName : ''));
            track.append(b);
          });
        }
        ruler.setAttribute('aria-valuemin', '0'); ruler.setAttribute('aria-valuemax', String(day.hours.length - 1)); ruler.setAttribute('aria-valuenow', String(selectedIndex)); ruler.setAttribute('aria-valuetext', dateLabel(localDate) + ' ' + row.at);
        [...track.children].forEach((b, i) => b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(i === selectedIndex)));
        calendarMonth();
        grid.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(b => b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(b.dataset.date === localDate)));
      }
      function setIndex(i, phase) { selectedIndex = clamp(i); render(); onChange(state(), phase); if (phase !== 'preview') settle(selectedIndex); }
      function cancel() {
        if (!drag) { stopMotion(); paint(selectedIndex); return; }
        const old = drag.index; drag = null; suppressClick = true; setIndex(old, 'cancel');
      }
      function chooseDay(i) {
        if (i < 0 || i >= days.length || !days[i].hours.length) return;
        cancel();
        const clock = state().row.at;
        selectedDay = i;
        const sameClock = days[i].hours.findIndex(r => r.at === clock);
        selectedIndex = sameClock >= 0 ? sameClock : 0;
        render(); paint(selectedIndex); onChange(state(), 'commit');
      }
      function chooseDate(date) {
        cancel();
        const candidates = samples.filter(s => s.date === date);
        if (!candidates.length) return;
        const sameClock = candidates.find(s => s.clock === state().row.at), chosen = sameClock || candidates[0];
        selectedDay = chosen.dayIndex; selectedIndex = chosen.index;
        render(); paint(selectedIndex); onChange(state(), 'commit');
        if (!sameClock) get('night-time-label').textContent += ' · 已选可用时刻';
      }
      listen(root, 'click', e => {
        const button = e.target.closest('button'); if (!button) return;
        if (button.dataset.time != null) { if (!suppressClick) setIndex(Number(button.dataset.time), 'commit'); return; }
        if (button.id === 'date-prev') chooseDate(dates[dates.indexOf(state().localDate)-1]);
        if (button.id === 'date-next') chooseDate(dates[dates.indexOf(state().localDate)+1]);
        if (button.id === 'date-today') chooseDay(days.findIndex(d => d.offset === 0));
        if (button.id === 'date-open') { cancel(); calendar.showModal(); grid.querySelector(`[data-date="${state().localDate}"]`)?.focus(); }
      });
      listen(grid, 'click', e => { const b = e.target.closest('button[data-date]'); if (!b || b.disabled) return; chooseDate(b.dataset.date); calendar.close(); });
      listen(calendar.querySelector('#calendar-close'), 'click', () => calendar.close());
      listen(calendar, 'click', e => { const b = calendar.getBoundingClientRect(); if (e.target === calendar && (e.clientX < b.left || e.clientX > b.right || e.clientY < b.top || e.clientY > b.bottom)) calendar.close(); });
      listen(calendar, 'close', () => get('date-open').focus({preventScroll:true}));
      listen(ruler, 'pointerdown', e => {
        if (!e.isPrimary || (e.pointerType === 'mouse' && e.button !== 0)) { cancel(); return; }
        if (drag) cancel();
        stopMotion(); suppressClick = false; drag = { id:e.pointerId, x:e.clientX, y:e.clientY, index:selectedIndex, origin:presented, axis:null, progress:presented };
      });
      listen(ruler, 'pointermove', e => {
        if (!drag || e.pointerId !== drag.id) return;
        const dx = e.clientX - drag.x, dy = e.clientY - drag.y;
        if (!drag.axis && Math.max(Math.abs(dx), Math.abs(dy)) > 6) drag.axis = Math.abs(dx) > Math.abs(dy) ? 'x' : 'y';
        if (drag.axis === 'y') suppressClick = true;
        if (drag.axis !== 'x') return;
        ruler.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); suppressClick = true; drag.progress = clamp(drag.origin - dx / 66);
        setIndex(Math.round(drag.progress), 'preview'); paint(drag.progress);
      });
      listen(ruler, 'pointerup', () => { const ended = drag; drag = null; if (ended?.axis === 'x') setIndex(Math.round(ended.progress), 'commit'); else if (ended) settle(selectedIndex); });
      listen(ruler, 'pointercancel', cancel);
      listen(ruler, 'lostpointercapture', () => { if (drag) cancel(); });
      listen(window, 'pointerup', () => { if (drag && drag.axis !== 'x') drag = null; });
      listen(ruler, 'keydown', e => {
        if (e.key === 'Escape') { if (drag) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); cancel(); } return; }
        if (!['ArrowLeft','ArrowRight','Home','End'].includes(e.key)) return;
        e.preventDefault(); if (drag) cancel(); else stopMotion(); suppressClick = false;
        setIndex(e.key === 'Home' ? 0 : e.key === 'End' ? days[selectedDay].hours.length - 1 : selectedIndex + (e.key === 'ArrowRight' ? 1 : -1), 'commit');
      });
      listen(document, 'visibilitychange', () => { if (document.hidden) { cancel(); stopMotion(); paint(selectedIndex); } });
      listen(reducedMotion, 'change', () => { if (reducedMotion.matches) { stopMotion(); paint(selectedIndex); } });
      render(); paint(selectedIndex);
      return { cancel() { cancel(); stopMotion(); paint(selectedIndex); }, getState: state, destroy() { cancel(); stopMotion(); listeners.abort(); calendar.remove(); } };
    }
  };
})();


## docs/design-resources/wechat-miniapp/map/adopted/layer-selector/preview/interaction.js
// All date/sky/weather records are the existing isolated design samples.
// This page does not request a real MapScene or infer map polygons.
(() => {
  const check = '<svg width="11" height="11" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="white" stroke-width="3" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m4 12 5 5 11-11" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/></svg>';
  const cards = [...document.querySelectorAll('[data-layer]')];
  const toggle = document.querySelector('#layer-toggle'), panel = document.querySelector('#layer-panel');
  let selectedLayer = 'TOTAL_CLOUD';
  cards.forEach(card => {
    card.lastElementChild.className = 'layer-check';
    const text = card.children[1]; text.children[0].className = 'layer-title'; text.children[1].className = 'layer-subtitle';
    const light = card.dataset.layer === 'LIGHT';
    const icon = document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg', 'svg');
    icon.setAttribute('viewBox','0 0 24 24'); icon.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true'); icon.classList.add('layer-icon');
    icon.innerHTML = light ? '<path d="M9 18h6m-5 3h4M8 14a6 6 0 1 1 8 0c-1 .8-1 1.8-1 2H9c0-.2 0-1.2-1-2Z"/>' : '<path d="M7 18a4 4 0 0 1-.7-7.94A6 6 0 0 1 18 9a4.5 4.5 0 0 1 .5 9Z"/>';
    const label = document.createElement('span'); label.textContent = light ? '光污染' : '云量';
    text.children[0].replaceChildren(icon,label); card.setAttribute('aria-label',label.textContent);
  });
  function render() {
    const cloud = selectedLayer === 'TOTAL_CLOUD';
    const region = document.querySelector('#layer-time-region');
    region.inert = !cloud;
    region.setAttribute('aria-hidden', String(!cloud));
    document.querySelector('#layer-source').textContent = cloud ? '云量预报 · 仅覆盖有数据区域' : '年度夜光估算 · 不随观测时间变化';
    const items = selectedLayer === 'LIGHT'
      ? [['#87714A','相对较低'],['#BE9B61','相对中等'],['#E0C998','相对较高']]
      : [['#4E8E9B','少云 0–30%'],['#76AAB2','中等 31–65%'],['#A9C9CE','多云 66–100%']];
    document.querySelector('#layer-legend').innerHTML = items.map(([color,text]) => '<span><i style="background:'+color+'"></i>'+text+'</span>').join('');
    cards.forEach(card => { const active = card.dataset.layer === selectedLayer; card.setAttribute('aria-checked', String(active)); card.tabIndex = active ? 0 : -1; card.lastElementChild.innerHTML = active ? check : ''; });
    document.body.dataset.selectedLayer = selectedLayer;
  }
  window.layerObservationTime = window.StarwardObservationTime.mount(document.querySelector('#layer-time'), {
    days:window.designDays, dayIndex:7, index:6,
    onChange:render
  });
  function select(card) { if (selectedLayer === card.dataset.layer) return; window.layerObservationTime.cancel(); selectedLayer = card.dataset.layer; card.focus({preventScroll:true}); render(); }
  cards.forEach(card => {
    card.addEventListener('click', () => select(card));
    card.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
      if (!['ArrowLeft','ArrowRight','Home','End'].includes(e.key)) return;
      e.preventDefault();
      const next = e.key === 'Home' ? cards[0] : e.key === 'End' ? cards[1] : cards.find(c => c !== card);
      select(next); next.focus();
    });
  });
  function open(value) {
    window.layerObservationTime.cancel(); panel.hidden = !value; toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded',String(value)); document.body.dataset.layerOpen = String(value);
    if (!value) toggle.focus({preventScroll:true});
  }
  toggle.addEventListener('click', () => open(panel.hidden));
  document.querySelector('.map-bg').addEventListener('click', () => open(false));
  document.addEventListener('keydown', e => { if (e.key === 'Escape' && !document.querySelector('dialog[open]')) { e.preventDefault(); open(false); } });
  // Keep the existing design components available for direct review.
  document.querySelector('#primary-nav').setAttribute('aria-label','主导航（静态背景）');
  render();
})();


## docs/design-resources/wechat-miniapp/map/revisions/three-requirements-2026-09-13/preview/layers.js
(()=>{const source=document.querySelector('#layer-source');const row=document.createElement('label');row.className='terrain-layer-choice';row.innerHTML='<img alt="" src="/docs/design-resources/wechat-miniapp/shared/icons/adopted/b-matte-256/assets/terrain--day--default.png"><span>地形<small>叠加高程阴影 · 可与上方图层组合</small></span><input id="terrain-toggle" type="checkbox">';document.querySelector('#layer-time-region').before(row);function publish(){parent.postMessage({type:'terrain-layer-change',layer:document.body.dataset.selectedLayer,terrain:row.querySelector('input').checked},location.origin);}row.onchange=publish;new MutationObserver(publish).observe(document.body,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['data-selected-layer']});document.querySelectorAll('[data-layer]').forEach(card=>{const svg=card.querySelector('.layer-icon');const img=document.createElement('img');img.className='layer-icon';img.alt='';img.src='/docs/design-resources/wechat-miniapp/shared/icons/adopted/b-matte-256/assets/'+(card.dataset.layer==='LIGHT'?'bulb':'cloud')+'--day--default.png';svg.replaceWith(img);});publish();})();
